## Runtime and world-pack coverage

`pct_wildlife.gd`, `wildlife_actor.gd`, wildlife population and insect scripts define regional pools, height bands, day windows, idle/move/startled clips and deterministic cell spawning. Unreal imports all 39 roster entries (shared source files counted once), retains clips and adds Sequencer wander plus Niagara mesh swarms. Static scatter uses HISM/InstancedFoliageActor.

World-pack draft inspected at origin/work/tb-worldpack-v2 `b96f0632424b2bfd89852c4d199610450668c822`. Adapter emits actual `thru.chunk-placements/v2`, explicit instances with Y-up local coordinates, yaw quaternions, scales, provenance and seed descriptor. Schema validation passed for 44,263 placements. Scatter strata remain empty because the generator does not claim the draft xorshift algorithm.
